00210: # All major semantic owners and their dependent components retained.
00211: # Architecture/camera anchors fixed; no geometry changed to adjust appearance.

00222: def add_surface_variation(slot,scale,strength,distance):
00223:     marker=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00224:     assert marker is not None,slot
00225:     meshes=[o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00226:     assert meshes,slot
00227:     for ob in meshes:
00228:         if not ob.data.materials:
00229:             continue
00230:         ob.data=ob.data.copy()
00231:         for index,material in enumerate(list(ob.data.materials)):
00232:             if material is None:
00233:                 continue
00234:             mat=material.copy()
00235:             ob.data.materials[index]=mat
00236:             mat.use_nodes=True
00237:             nt=mat.node_tree
00238:             bsdf=next((n for n in nt.nodes if n.type=='BSDF_PRINCIPLED'),None)
00239:             if bsdf is None:
00240:                 continue
00241:             bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.83
00242:             noise=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00243:             noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=scale
00244:             noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00245:             bump=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00246:             bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=strength
00247:             bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=distance
00248:             nt.links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00249:             nt.links.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bsdf.inputs['Normal'])
00250:

00059: # Each bunk owns posts, feet, mattress supports, bedding, guards and ladder.
00060: # Actual mattress support heights are lower/upper, independent of rail AABBs.
00061: def make_bunk(name, x0, yc, length=None, width=None):

00063:     width = width or BED['width']

00072:                                           color=C['metal']))

00204: # Restrained procedural variation, scoped through known semantic markers.
00205: # Material copies preserve other owners even when SceneAct shares finishes.
00206: def add_surface_variation(slot, scale, strength, distance):
00207:     marker=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00208:     assert marker is not None, slot
00209:     meshes=[o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00210:     assert meshes, slot
00211:     for ob in meshes:
00212:         if not ob.data.materials:
00213:             continue
00214:         ob.data=ob.data.copy()
00215:         for index,material in enumerate(list(ob.data.materials)):
00216:             if material is None:
00217:                 continue
00218:             mat=material.copy()
00219:             ob.data.materials[index]=mat
00220:             mat.use_nodes=True
00221:             nt=mat.node_tree
00222:             bsdf=next((n for n in nt.nodes if n.type=='BSDF_PRINCIPLED'),None)
00223:             if bsdf is None:
00224:                 continue
00225:             bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.83
00226:             noise=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00227:             noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=scale
00228:             noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00229:             bump=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00230:             bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=strength
00231:             bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=distance
00232:             nt.links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00233:             nt.links.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bsdf.inputs['Normal'])
00234: 
00235: add_surface_variation("architecture",8,.22,.035)
00236: add_surface_variation("fireplace",18,.35,.025)
00237: add_surface_variation("wardrobe",7,.17,.018)

00213: def add_surface_variation(slot,scale,strength,distance):
00214:     marker=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00215:     assert marker is not None,slot
00216:     meshes=[o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00217:     assert meshes,slot
00218:     for ob in meshes:
00219:         if not ob.data.materials:
00220:             continue
00221:         ob.data=ob.data.copy()
00222:         for index,material in enumerate(list(ob.data.materials)):
00223:             if material is None:
00224:                 continue
00225:             mat=material.copy()
00226:             ob.data.materials[index]=mat
00227:             mat.use_nodes=True
00228:             nt=mat.node_tree
00229:             bsdf=next((n for n in nt.nodes if n.type=='BSDF_PRINCIPLED'),None)
00230:             if bsdf is None:
00231:                 continue
00232:             bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.83
00233:             noise=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00234:             noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=scale
00235:             noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00236:             bump=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00237:             bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=strength
00238:             bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=distance
00239:             nt.links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00240:             nt.links.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bsdf.inputs['Normal'])
00241:

00221: 
00222: def add_surface_variation(slot,scale,strength,distance):
00223:     marker=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00224:     assert marker is not None,slot
00225:     meshes=[o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00226:     assert meshes,slot
00227:     for ob in meshes:
00228:         if not ob.data.materials:
00229:             continue
00230:         ob.data=ob.data.copy()
00231:         for index,material in enumerate(list(ob.data.materials)):
00232:             if material is None:
00233:                 continue
00234:             mat=material.copy()
00235:             ob.data.materials[index]=mat
00236:             mat.use_nodes=True
00237:             nt=mat.node_tree
00238:             bsdf=next((n for n in nt.nodes if n.type=='BSDF_PRINCIPLED'),None)
00239:             if bsdf is None:
00240:                 continue
00241:             bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.83
00242:             noise=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00243:             noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=scale
00244:             noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00245:             bump=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00246:             bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=strength
00247:             bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=distance
00248:             nt.links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00249:             nt.links.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bsdf.inputs['Normal'])
00250:

00010: # Reference left window x0:90; render window approximately x0:90.
00011: # Back shelves remain nearly horizontal; right returns rise toward the right.
00012: # Reference bench crops the bottom and sides, occluding lower room inventory.
00013: # Render retains these crops but shows a larger empty strip behind the bench.
00014: # Measured facts: steps 1 and 2 returned valid images; observed execution times
00015: # were 32.652 and 34.119 seconds. No geometric calibration was measured.
00016: #
00017: # CHOSEN FAMILY: camera only. Modestly increase focal length, lower the camera
00018: # and lower its target. Prediction: crop the empty upper band, spread the far
00019: # shelf levels vertically, and retain approximately the kettle's vertical position.
00020: # Three independent anchors motivating this change: upper wall crop, far shelf
00021: # spacing, and near kettle/bench contact. Horizontal framing remains uncertain.
00022: # Geometry, lighting, finishes, semantic owners and support planes stay fixed.
00023: # Render 2 has improved dark/light ordering, though inventory remains visually
00024: # simplified and the foreground less crowded than the photograph.
00025: #
00026: # Ownership/support ledger:
00027: # architecture -> room(): plaster, floor, window panes and attached frame.
00028: # shelves -> boards/brackets; explicit levels[] are actual back/right board tops.
00029: # plane slots -> plane(): stock, iron, knob and tote share one editable Asset.

00031: # left_rack slots -> rack rail and its attached handles/blades.

00055:     return S.include(name, node)

00057: def cube(owner, name, size, center, color):
00058:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=center, color=color))
00059: 
00060: def beam(owner, name, a, b, width, depth, color):
00061:     dx, dz = b[0]-a[0], b[2]-a[2]
00062:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)
00063:     n = box((width,depth,length),
00064:             center=((a[0]+b[0])/2,(a[1]+b[1])/2,(a[2]+b[2])/2),
00065:             color=color)
00066:     n.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx,dz)))
00067:     return owner.include(name,n)

00104: def plane(name, position, length=.38, width=.13, height=.12,
00105:           angle=0, wood=True, detail=True):

00026:     "wall": (.71,.73,.68), "wood": (.39,.20,.075),
00027:     "honey": (.59,.33,.12), "edge": (.67,.42,.19),
00028:     "darkwood": (.22,.115,.055), "iron": (.105,.115,.108),

00036: def add(name, node):
00037:     return S.include(name, node)
00038: 
00039: def cube(owner, name, size, center, color):
00040:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=center, color=color))
00041: 
00042: def beam(owner, name, a, b, width, depth, color):
00043:     # Rectangular beam in the XZ plane, useful for flat wall-mounted tools.
00044:     dx, dz = b[0]-a[0], b[2]-a[2]
00045:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)
00046:     n = box((width,depth,length),
00047:             center=((a[0]+b[0])/2,(a[1]+b[1])/2,(a[2]+b[2])/2),
00048:             color=color)
00049:     n.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx,dz)))
00050:     return owner.include(name,n)
00051: 
00052: def room():

00054:     cube(a,"back_plaster",(5.65,.12,4.3),(0,2.19,2.05),C["wall"])

00081: def plane(name, position, length=.38, width=.13, height=.12, angle=0,
00082:           wood=True, detail=True):
00083:     a = Asset(name)
00084:     col = rng.choice([C["wood"],C["honey"],C["darkwood"]])
00085:     cube(a,"stock",(length,width,height),(0,0,height/2),col if wood else C["iron"])
00086:     if not wood:
00087:         cube(a,"bright_sole",(length+.015,width+.015,.018),(0,0,.014),C["steel"])
00088:     blade = box((.045,width*.82,height*.95),center=(.01,0,height*.95),color=C["iron"])
00089:     blade.turn('y',-28)
00090:     a.include("slanted_cutter",blade)
00091:     if detail:
00092:         a.include("front_knob",ball(width*.23,center=(-length*.30,0,height+.035),color=col))
00093:         # Angular tote preserves the characteristic open upper silhouette.
00094:         a.include("tote_back",rod(.018,start=(length*.28,0,height),
00095:                                   end=(length*.34,0,height+.12),color=col))
00096:         a.include("tote_top",rod(.018,start=(length*.34,0,height+.12),
00097:                                  end=(length*.13,0,height+.13),color=col))
00098:     a.turn('z',angle,pivot=(0,0,0))
00099:     a.move(position)
00100:     add(name,a)

00269: # Pre-execution self-review:
00270: # All major inventory families are represented with separate editable owners.
00271: # Shelf contact is at explicit board-top Z; bench contact is at BENCH_Z.
00272: # Top-square arrangement and small tool identities remain visual hypotheses.
00273: # Repetition expands to roughly 600-700 simple meshes; most cost is shelving
00274: # inventory, with only 32 loose nails and one five-point curved kettle bail.
00275: # No fillet expansion. All dimensions are positive; rods have distinct endpoints;
00276: # every loop uses unique include slots. No post-realize geometry or material scans.
00277: # Camera, lighting and world follow realize; materials are set at construction.
00278: realize(S)
00279: set_view(**CAMERA)
00280: scene=bpy.context.scene
00281: if scene.world is None:
00282:     scene.world=bpy.data.worlds.new("World")
00283: scene.world.use_nodes=True
00284: scene.world.node_tree.nodes["Background"].inputs["Color"].default_value=(.62,.69,.72,1)
00285: scene.world.node_tree.nodes["Background"].inputs["Strength"].default_value=.45
00286: area_emitter((-3.1,-.6,3.4),power=850,size=3.0,target=(0,1,1.2),color=(.79,.90,1))
00287: area_emitter((.2,-2.3,4.2),power=650,size=4,target=(0,1.0,1.4),color=(1,.91,.76))
